- **Misread resolved (drill-lens lesson, no fault)**: at waking start I combined `ls peer/inbox/squall/ ...` and `ls processed/ | tail -5` in one bash call; processed/'s 5 newest entries (the 00:18–00:31Z Oct-4 deliveries) printed directly under the empty `peer/inbox/squall/:` header, and I briefly read that as 5 squall/-subdir messages that had then vanished.
